"
|                     <p>
|                       id="mwEw"
|                       "A "
|                       <b>
|                         id="mwFA"
|                         "web browser"
|                       ", often abbreviated as "
|                       <b>
|                         id="mwFQ"
|                         "browser"
|                       ", is an "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Application_software"
|                         id="mwFg"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Application software"
|                         "application"
|                       " for accessing "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Website"
|                         id="mwFw"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Website"
|                         "websites"
|                       ". When a "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/User_(computing)"
|                         id="mwGA"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="User (computing)"
|                         "user"
|                       " requests a "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_page"
|                         id="mwGQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Web page"
|                         "web page"
|                       " from a particular website, the browser retrieves its "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Computer_file"
|                         id="mwGg"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Computer file"
|                         "files"
|                       " from a "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_server"
|                         id="mwGw"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Web server"
|                         "web server"
|                       " and then displays the page on the user's screen. Browsers can also display content stored locally on the user's device."
|                     "

"
|                     <p>
|                       id="mwHA"
|                       "Browsers are used on a range of devices, including "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Desktop_computer"
|                         id="mwHQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Desktop computer"

|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Laptop"
|                         id="mwHg"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Laptop"
|                         "laptops"
|                       ", "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tablet_computer"
|                         id="mwHw"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Tablet computer"
|                         "tablets"
|                       ", "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Smartphone"
|                         id="mwIA"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Smartphone"
|                         "smartphones"
|                       ", "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Smartwatch"
|                         id="mwIQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Smartwatch"
|                         "smartwatches"
|                       ", "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Smart_TV"
|                         id="mwIg"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Smart TV"
|                         "smart televisions"
|                       " and "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Video_game_console"
|                         id="mwIw"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Video game console"
|                         "consoles"
|                       "."

"
|                     <p>
|                       id="mwTA"
|                       "The purpose of a web browser is to fetch content and display it on the user's device."
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt22"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-5"}}"
|                         id="cite_ref-5"
|                         rel="dc:references"
|                         typeof="mw:Extension/ref"
|                         <a>
|                           href="#cite_note-5"
|                           id="mwTQ"
|                           <span>
|                             class="mw-reflink-text"
|                             id="mwTg"
|                             <span>
|                               class="cite-bracket"
|                               id="mwTw"
|                               "["
|                             "5"
|                             <span>
|                               class="cite-bracket"
|                               id="mwUA"
|                               "]"
|                       " This process begins when the user inputs a "
|                       <a>
|                         class="mw-redirect"
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uniform_Resource_Locator"
|                         id="mwUQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Uniform Resource Locator"
|                         "Uniform Resource Locator"
|                       " (URL), such as "
|                       <i>
|                         id="mwUg"
|                         <code>
|                           id="mwUw"
|                           <span>
|                             id="mwVA"
|                             typeof="mw:Nowiki"
|                             "https://en.wikipedia.org/"
|                       ", into the browser's "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Address_bar"
|                         id="mwVQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Address bar"
|                         "address bar"
|                       ". Virtually all URLs on "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Wide_Web"
|                         id="mwVg"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="World Wide Web"
|                         "the Web"
|                       " start with either "
|                       <i>
|                         id="mwVw"
|                         <code>
|                           id="mwWA"
|                           "http:"
|                       " or "
|                       <i>
|                         id="mwWQ"
|                         <code>
|                           id="mwWg"
|                           "https:"
|                       " which means they are retrieved with the "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTTP"
|                         id="mwWw"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="HTTP"
|                         "Hypertext Transfer Protocol"
|                       " (HTTP). For "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTTPS"
|                         id="mwXA"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="HTTPS"
|                         "secure mode"
|                       " (HTTPS), the connection between the browser and "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_server"
|                         id="mwXQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Web server"
|                         "web server"
|                       " is "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Encryption"
|                         id="mwXg"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Encryption"
|                         "encrypted"
|                       ", providing a "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Communications_security"
|                         id="mwXw"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Communications security"
|                         "secure"
|                       " and "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Information_privacy"
|                         id="mwYA"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Information privacy"
|                         "private"
|                       " data transfer."
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt25"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-6"}}"
|                         id="cite_ref-6"

"
|                     <p>
|                       id="mwfg"
|                       "Web pages usually contain "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hyperlink"
|                         id="mwfw"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Hyperlink"
|                         "hyperlinks"
|                       " to other pages and resources. Each link contains a URL, and when it is "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Point_and_click"
|                         id="mwgA"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Point and click"
|                         "clicked"
|                       " or "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Touchscreen"
|                         id="mwgQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Touchscreen"
|                         "tapped"
|                       ", the browser navigates to the new resource. Most browsers use an internal "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_cache"
|                         id="mwgg"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Web cache"
|                         "cache"
|                       " of web page resources to improve loading times for subsequent visits to the same page. The cache can store many items, such as large images, so they do not need to be downloaded from the server again. Cached items are usually only stored for as long as the web server stipulates in its HTTP response messages."

"
|                     <p>
|                       id="mwng"
|                       "The first web browser, called "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/WorldWideWeb"
|                         id="mwnw"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="WorldWideWeb"
|                         "WorldWideWeb"
|                       ", was created in 1990 by Sir "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tim_Berners-Lee"
|                         id="mwoA"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Tim Berners-Lee"
|                         "Tim Berners-Lee"
|                       "."
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt57"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-16"}}"
|                         id="cite_ref-16"
|                         rel="dc:references"
|                         typeof="mw:Extension/ref"
|                         <a>
|                           href="#cite_note-16"

|                               class="cite-bracket"
|                               id="mwow"
|                               "["
|                             "16"
|                             <span>
|                               class="cite-bracket"
|                               id="mwpA"
|                               "]"
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt60"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-17"}}"
|                         id="cite_ref-17"

|                         typeof="mw:Extension/ref"

|                           id="mwpQ"

|                             class="mw-reflink-text"
|                             id="mwpg"
|                             <span>
|                               class="cite-bracket"

|                               "["
|                             "17"

|                               id="mwqA"
|                               "]"
|                       " He then recruited "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nicola_Pellow"
|                         id="mwqQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Nicola Pellow"
|                         "Nicola Pellow"
|                       " to write the "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Line_Mode_Browser"
|                         id="mwqg"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Line Mode Browser"
|                         "Line Mode Browser"
|                       ", which displayed web pages on "
|                       <a>
|                         class="mw-redirect"
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dumb_terminals"
|                         id="mwqw"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Dumb terminals"
|                         "dumb terminals"
|                       "."
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt63"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":5"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:5-18"}}"
|                         id="cite_ref-:5_18-0"
|                         rel="dc:references"
|                         typeof="mw:Extension/ref"
|                         <a>
|                           href="#cite_note-:5-18"
|                           id="mwrA"

|                               "["
|                             "18"
|                             <span>
|                               class="cite-bracket"
|                               id="mwrw"
|                               "]"
|                       " The "
|                       <a>
|                         class="mw-redirect"
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mosaic_(web_browser)"
|                         id="mwsA"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Mosaic (web browser)"
|                         "Mosaic"
|                       " web browser was released in April 1993, and was later credited as the first web browser to find mainstream popularity."
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt66"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-19"}}"

|                               "]"
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt69"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"bloomberg"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-bloomberg-20"}}"

|                           id="mwtQ"
|                           <span>
|                             class="mw-reflink-text"
|                             id="mwtg"
|                             <span>
|                               class="cite-bracket"
|                               id="mwtw"
|                               "["
|                             "20"
|                             <span>
|                               class="cite-bracket"
|                               id="mwuA"
|                               "]"
|                       " Its innovative "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Graphical_user_interface"
|                         id="mwuQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Graphical user interface"
|                         "graphical user interface"
|                       " made the "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Wide_Web"
|                         id="mwug"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="World Wide Web"
|                         "World Wide Web"
|                       " easy to navigate and thus more accessible to the average person. This, in turn, sparked the Internet boom of the 1990s, when the Web grew at a very rapid rate."
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt70"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"bloomberg"}}"
|                         id="cite_ref-bloomberg_20-1"

|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Netscape Navigator"
|                         "Netscape Navigator"
|                       " in 1994. Navigator quickly became the "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Usage_share_of_web_browsers#Older_reports_(pre-2000)"
|                         id="mwwQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Usage share of web browsers"
|                         "most popular"
|                       " browser."

|                         class="mw-ref reference"

"
|                     <p>
|                       id="mwxg"
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Microsoft"
|                         id="mwxw"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Microsoft"
|                         "Microsoft"
|                       " debuted "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet_Explorer"
|                         id="mwyA"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Internet Explorer"
|                         "Internet Explorer"
|                       " in 1995, leading to a "
|                       <a>
|                         class="mw-redirect"
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Browser_war"
|                         id="mwyQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Browser war"
|                         "browser war"
|                       " with Netscape. Within a few years, Microsoft gained a dominant position in the browser market for two reasons: it bundled Internet Explorer with its popular "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Microsoft_Windows"
|                         id="mwyg"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Microsoft Windows"
|                         "Windows"
|                       " "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Operating_system"
|                         id="mwyw"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Operating system"
|                         "operating system"
|                       " and did so as "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Freeware"
|                         id="mwzA"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Freeware"
|                         "freeware"
|                       " with no restrictions on usage. The market share of Internet Explorer peaked at over 95% in the early 2000s."
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt76"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"searchenginejournal.com"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-searchenginejournal.com-22"}}"
|                         id="cite_ref-searchenginejournal.com_22-0"
|                         rel="dc:references"
|                         typeof="mw:Extension/ref"
|                         <a>
|                           href="#cite_note-searchenginejournal.com-22"
|                           id="mwzQ"
|                           <span>
|                             class="mw-reflink-text"
|                             id="mwzg"
|                             <span>
|                               class="cite-bracket"
|                               id="mwzw"
|                               "["
|                             "22"
|                             <span>
|                               class="cite-bracket"
|                               id="mw0A"
|                               "]"
|                       " In 1998, Netscape launched what would become the "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mozilla_Foundation"
|                         id="mw0Q"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Mozilla Foundation"
|                         "Mozilla Foundation"
|                       " to create a new browser using the "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Open-source_software"
|                         id="mw0g"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Open-source software"
|                         "open-source software"
|                       " model. This work evolved into the "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Firefox"
|                         id="mw0w"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Firefox"
|                         "Firefox"
|                       " browser, first released by Mozilla in 2004. Firefox's market share peaked at 32% in 2010."
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt79"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-23"}}"

|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apple_Inc."
|                         id="mw2A"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Apple Inc."
|                         "Apple"

|                       " browser in 2003; it remains the dominant browser on Apple devices, though it did not become popular elsewhere."

"
|                     <p>
|                       id="mw9g"
|                       "Since the early 2000s, browsers have greatly expanded their "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTML"
|                         id="mw9w"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="HTML"
|                         "HTML"
|                       ", "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CSS"
|                         id="mw-A"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="CSS"
|                         "CSS"
|                       ", "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/JavaScript"
|                         id="mw-Q"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="JavaScript"
|                         "JavaScript"
|                       ", and "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multimedia"
|                         id="mw-g"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Multimedia"
|                         "multimedia"
|                       " capabilities. One reason has been to enable more sophisticated websites, such as "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_application"
|                         id="mw-w"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Web application"
|                         "web apps"
|                       ". Another factor is the significant increase of "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Broadband"
|                         id="mw_A"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Broadband"
|                         "broadband"
|                       " connectivity in "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_sovereign_states_by_number_of_broadband_Internet_subscriptions"
|                         id="mw_Q"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="List of sovereign states by number of broadband Internet subscriptions"
|                         "many parts"
|                       " of the world, enabling people to access data-intensive content, such as "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Streaming_media"
|                         id="mw_g"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Streaming media"
|                         "streaming"
|                       " "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/High-definition_video"
|                         id="mw_w"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="High-definition video"
|                         "HD video"
|                       " on "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/YouTube"
|                         id="mwAQA"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="YouTube"
|                         "YouTube"
|                       ", that was not possible during the era of "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dial-up_Internet_access"
|                         id="mwAQE"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Dial-up Internet access"
|                         "dial-up modems"
|                       "."
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt95"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-28"}}"
|                         id="cite_ref-28"
|                         rel="dc:references"
|                         typeof="mw:Extension/ref"
|                         <a>
|                           href="#cite_note-28"
|                           id="mwAQI"
|                           <span>
|                             class="mw-reflink-text"
|                             id="mwAQM"
|                             <span>
|                               class="cite-bracket"
|                               id="mwAQQ"
|                               "["
|                             "28"
|                             <span>
|                               class="cite-bracket"

"
|                     <p>
|                       id="mwAQY"
|                       "Starting in the mid-2020s, browsers with integrated "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Artificial_intelligence"
|                         id="mwAQc"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Artificial intelligence"
|                         "artificial intelligence"
|                       " (AI) capabilities, known as "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AI_browser"
|                         id="mwAQg"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="AI browser"
|                         "AI browsers"
|                       ", have become increasingly common. This includes both new entrants to the browser market, such as "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comet_(browser)"
|                         id="mwAQk"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Comet (browser)"
|                         "Perplexity Comet"
|                       " and "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ChatGPT_Atlas"
|                         id="mwAQo"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="ChatGPT Atlas"
|                         "ChatGPT Atlas"
|                       ", and established browsers that added AI features, such as Chrome with the "
|                       <a>
|                         class="mw-redirect"
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gemini_(chatbot)"
|                         id="mwAQs"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"

|                     <p>
|                       id="mwAV0"
|                       "Access to some web content — particularly "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Streaming_media"

|                       <a>
|                         class="mw-redirect"
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Digital_Rights_Management"
|                         id="mwAV8"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Digital Rights Management"
|                         "Digital Rights Management"
|                       " (DRM) software. A web browser is able to access DRM-restricted content through the use of a "
|                       <a>
|                         class="mw-redirect"
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Content_Decryption_Module"
|                         id="mwAWA"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Content Decryption Module"
|                         "Content Decryption Module"
|                       " (CDM) such as "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Widevine"
|                         id="mwAWE"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Widevine"
|                         "Widevine"
|                       ". As of 2020, the CDMs used by dominant web browsers require browser providers to pay costly license fees, making it unfeasible for most independent open-source browsers to offer access to DRM-restricted content."

|                     <p>
|                       id="mwAYI"
|                       "The other two browsers in the top four are made from different "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Codebase"
|                         id="mwAYM"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Codebase"
|                         "codebases"
|                       ". "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Safari_(web_browser)"
|                         id="mwAYQ"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Safari (web browser)"
|                         "Safari"
|                       ", based on "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apple_Inc."
|                         id="mwAYU"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Apple Inc."
|                         "Apple"
|                       "'s "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/WebKit"
|                         id="mwAYY"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="WebKit"
|                         "WebKit"
|                       " code, is the second most popular web browser and is dominant on Apple devices, resulting in a 16% global share."
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt134"
|                         class="mw-ref reference"
|                         data-mw="{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"statcounter"}}"
|                         id="cite_ref-statcounter_2-5"
|                         rel="dc:references"
|                         typeof="mw:Extension/ref"
|                         <a>
|                           href="#cite_note-statcounter-2"
|                           id="mwAYc"

|                               "["

|                       " "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Firefox"
|                         id="mwAYs"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Firefox"
|                         "Firefox"
|                       ", in fourth place, with about 2% market share,"
|                       <sup>
|                         about="#mwt135"

|                         "Mozilla"
|                       "'s code. Both of these codebases are open-source, so a number of small niche browsers are also made from them."

|                   <section>

|                     "
"
|                     <span>
|                       about="#mwt171"

|                         about="#mwt172"

|                     <div>
|                       about="#mwt171"

|                       <a>

|                     "
"
|                     <p>
|                       id="mwAmo"
|                       "Web browsers are popular targets for "
|                       <a>

|                       " to steal information, destroy "

|                         id="mwAm0"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Computer file"
|                         "files"

|                         id="mwAm4"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Malware"
|                         "malicious"
|                       " activities. Browser vendors regularly patch these security holes, so users are strongly encouraged to keep their browser software updated. Other protection measures are "
|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antivirus_software"
|                         id="mwAm8"
|                         rel="mw:WikiLink"
|                         title="Antivirus software"

|                       <a>
|                         href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet_fraud"

|                         title="Internet fraud"
|                         "scams"
|                       "."

|                       <span>
